3. Glu acts as proton acceptor during various steps

So the key distinction is:
- Lys-133: forms the Schiff base intermediate (initially with PLP as internal aldimine, and is involved in the transaldimination process where the substrate forms a Schiff base)
- Glu-45: acts as the proton acceptor (general base)

This matches option A: &quot;Glu-45 acts as the proton acceptor; Lys-133 participates in the substrate Schiff-base intermediate.&quot;

The key mechanistic point is that in PLP-dependent enzymes, the lysine is essential for forming the Schiff base (aldimine) intermediate - both the internal aldimine with PLP and facilitating the external aldimine with substrate. The glutamate acts as a general base/proton acceptor.

So the answer is A.
</pre>

</details>
